cigar-shaped object, ret�rned to residences in Staten Island, N.Y.
they were eftected by many after affects associated with close
9bservable sightings. Both witnesseS:·atated and indicated a possible

strange occurrence the night prior to the sighting which involved an.
eerie beacon-type light hovering above their tent; hostile-type
reaction from their own pets; severe sickness, with many symptoms,
which may be attributed to "electromagnetic sickness"; frequent
recurring dreams of events during their fleeing in t heir auto and
being pursued\ by the large:- UFO; obcession into the UFO phenomena;
appearance of, more than usual, ESP abilities; and possibly, proof
of an actual abduction while being pursu�a\ by the UFO { time loss ) .

                 force and 'blinding light . The officer became unconscious and awoke·
                 later in a daze . The vehicle's win dshield was shattered in four
                 s eparate locations; gla s s from one headlight wa s broken, yet, the
                 chrome edging r emainsin tact; and the CB/ police antenna was bent

                 of unaccountable time, and it was found that the vehicle had skidded
                 for 99 feet in a s traight.line, while only traveling at a speed of
                 48 m . p.h .
